Codex Response 10 / commentary

2026-06-24 08:53:45 +0800
我现在可以给出总结了：代码层面没有发现 NRT 直接链接 RT/Orocos；当前真实可执行的 NRT 集成主要是 HMI 到 WorkOffset/Tooling 的同进程 adapter。NRT 到 RT 的预期路径已经在文档和 SDK/RT 两侧合约中成形，但 ProgramEngine/Scheduler 这一段还没实现成代码模块。

Codex Response 11 / final_answer

2026-06-24 08:54:32 +0800
当前结论：MetaNC 现在的 **NRT/RT 联结边界设计已经明确**，但 **NRT 执行管线到 RT 的完整代码链还没落地**。已经落地最清楚的是 `rt-sdk`/`rt-api-contract`/RT `RtApi` 这条边界；NRT 侧目前更多是 HMI 与 NRT 域模块的同进程集成。

**目标结构**
- 设计上的链路是：`ProgramEngine / NC Kernel / Scheduler` 这类 NRT 执行管线生成指令，由 `Instruction Execution Scheduler` 通过 `rt-sdk` 下发到 RT。
- `rt-sdk` 是唯一稳定 userland-to-RT 边界，不应直接链接 `rt/Core` 或 `rt/Orocos`。
- `rt-api-contract` 是共享 wire contract，RT 把内部 Core/Orocos 状态映射成这些 contract type，SDK 再映射成用户 API 模型。

证据：
- [architecture.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/docs/dev/architecture.md:145)
- [rt-api-contract README](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt-api-contract/README.md:6)
- [rt-sdk CMakeLists](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt-sdk/CMakeLists.txt:10)

**NRT 当前实际状态**
- `nrt/program_engine` 目前是规格/文档状态：它定义为长驻 NRT daemon，HMI 是 client；它会协调 parser、kernel、scheduler，并把 RT-side commanding-loss safety 委托给 RT SDK。
- 但当前仓库里没有实际的 `nrt/nc_kernel`、`nrt/instruction_execution_scheduler`、`nrt/motion_algorithms` 目录；所以“G-code 到调度器再到 RT SDK”的完整 NRT 执行实现还不存在。
- HMI 现在真正接起来的是 NRT 域模块：`tooling_management` 和 `work_offset` 通过 `nrt/hmi/server/CMakeLists.txt` 以 `add_subdirectory` + adapter 方式进 HMI server，这是同进程 NRT 域集成，不是 RT 通讯模型。

证据：
- [MODULE-MODEL.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/nrt/program_engine/docs/spec/MODULE-MODEL.md:14)
- [nrt/hmi/server/CMakeLists.txt](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/nrt/hmi/server/CMakeLists.txt:84)
- [nrt/hmi/server/CMakeLists.txt](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/nrt/hmi/server/CMakeLists.txt:131)

**RT/SDK 当前实际状态**
- `rt-sdk` 已有 `Machine` facade、`IRtBackend`、OPC UA backend、quick-start、contract runner 和测试。
- `meta_nc_rt_sdk` 只链接 `meta_nc::rt_api`；OPC UA backend 额外链接 `meta_nc::rt_api_opcua`、`open62541pp`、`open62541`。
- `Machine::connect()` 先创建 backend，再 refresh topology/mode；OPC UA backend 打开 command/monitor 两个 client 连接。

证据：
- [machine.hpp](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt-sdk/include/meta_nc/rt_sdk/machine.hpp:10)
- [backend.hpp](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt-sdk/include/meta_nc/rt_sdk/backend.hpp:8)
- [machine.cpp](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt-sdk/src/machine.cpp:18)
- [opcua_backend.cpp](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt-sdk/src/opcua_backend.cpp:984)

**RT 内部如何暴露给 SDK**
- RT 的 Orocos deploy 脚本加载 `MachineBinding`、`MotionBank`、`DriveEndpointBank`、`SimServoBank`、`WorkingModeManager`、`ManualExecutor`、`AutomaticExecutor`、`RtApi`。
- 内部组件之间通过 Orocos peer/service/port 连接。
- 对外不是直接暴露 `MotionBank`，而是只通过 `rt_api.opcua_bind.exposeService(...)` 暴露 curated 服务：`system`、`mode`、`topology`、`resources`、`manual`、`automatic`、`mda`。
- `rt_api_types` 会把 `rt-api-contract` 里的 headers 复制给 typegen，生成 Orocos typekit，保证 OPC UA/TaskBrowser 边界能看到真实 wire types。

证据：
- [xyzac-spindle.ops](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt/Orocos/deploy/xyzac_spindle_sim/xyzac-spindle.ops:9)
- [xyzac-spindle.ops](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt/Orocos/deploy/xyzac_spindle_sim/xyzac-spindle.ops:130)
- [rt-api-component.hpp](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt/Orocos/src/meta_nc_rt_api/src/rt-api-component.hpp:22)
- [rt-api-component.cpp](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt/Orocos/src/meta_nc_rt_api/src/rt-api-component.cpp:69)
- [rt_api_types CMakeLists](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt/Orocos/src/rt_api_types/src/CMakeLists.txt:5)

**一句话总结**
现在的 MetaNC 是：**NRT 侧的 HMI/域模块已经有同进程集成；RT 侧已经有 `RtApi + OPC UA + rt-api-contract + rt-sdk` 的通信边界；但中间的 NRT 程序执行管线到 RT SDK 的真实调度实现还没形成完整代码链。** 另外，上轮实测发布版 `optimalcnc/metanc-rt:latest` 能启动，但当前 `rt-sdk` quick-start 连接它仍卡在 OPC UA `open_transport / BadConnectionClosed`，所以这条 live Docker 开发链路还不能算跑通。

Codex Response 1 / commentary

Codex Response 2 / commentary

2026-06-24 09:06:34 +0800
你这个判断方向是对的，但我要把范围说精确：仓库里已经有“ProgramEngine 对 HMI/外部观察者使用 ROS2 service/topic”的决策；还没有看到“RT 通讯代理服务层”，也就是一个少数模块调用 `rt-sdk`、多数 NRT 模块通过 ROS2 Services 间接访问 RT 的实现。

Codex Response 3 / final_answer

2026-06-24 09:06:50 +0800
是的，按你描述的架构意图看，**现在确实缺少这一层**。

更准确地说，缺的是一个 **NRT-side RT communication service / RT gateway daemon**：

```text
多数 NRT 模块
  -> ROS2 Services / Topics
  -> NRT RT 通讯服务层
  -> rt-sdk
  -> OPC UA / 其他 backend
  -> RT RtApi
  -> RT Core/Orocos
```

当前仓库状态我看到的是：

- `rt-sdk` 已经存在，是 userland 到 RT 的底层 SDK。
- `rt-api-contract` 已经存在，是 RT 与 SDK 共享的 wire contract。
- RT 侧 `RtApi` 已经存在，并通过 OPC UA 暴露 `system/mode/topology/resources/manual/automatic/mda`。
- `nrt/program_engine` 文档里已经决定 public seam 是 ROS2 services + lifecycle topic。
- 但没有看到一个实际的 ROS2 node/service 包，负责把 NRT 的 ROS2 请求翻译为 `rt-sdk::Machine` 调用。
- 也没有看到 `.srv/.action/.msg` 定义用于 RT 通讯服务层。
- 目前 `rt-sdk` 主要被 quick-start、how-to、contract runner 直接使用，不是被一个 NRT ROS2 gateway 包装起来供其他 NRT 模块调用。

所以架构缺口不是“RT SDK 没有”，而是：

```text
rt-sdk 之上的 NRT 服务化适配层缺失
```

这个层应该承担：
- 唯一或少数持有 `rt-sdk::Machine` connection 的进程；
- 暴露 ROS2 service/topic/action 给 ProgramEngine、HMI、diagnostics、scheduler；
- 屏蔽 OPC UA endpoint、重连、backend 选择、连接生命周期；
- 把 RT SDK 的 C++ API 映射成稳定 NRT ROS2 接口；
- 维护 topology/mode/resource/process-data cache；
- 把 RT 状态、命令回执、错误分类变成 NRT 可消费的消息。

我建议名字上不要叫 `rt-sdk`，而叫类似：
- `nrt/rt_gateway`
- `nrt/rt_comm_service`
- `nrt/machine_gateway`
- `nrt/rt_bridge`

如果按现有文档风格，最贴切可能是：

```text
/nrt/rt_gateway
```

它不是 RT 模块，也不是 SDK 本身，而是 NRT 内部的 ROS2-facing gateway。多数 NRT 模块不直接依赖 `rt-sdk`，只依赖这个 gateway 的 ROS2 contract。

Turn 5

User

2026-06-24 09:40:05 +0800
如果加入rt_gateway这一层之后，整个系统的架构、模块、通信 等模型关系大概是什么样子

Codex Response 1 / commentary

2026-06-24 09:40:36 +0800
我会把 `rt_gateway` 放在 NRT 侧，而不是放进 RT 或 `rt-sdk`：它是 ROS2 服务化适配层，内部才使用 `rt-sdk`。下面我按“模块关系、通信方向、职责边界、典型流程”给一个建议模型。

Codex Response 2 / final_answer

2026-06-24 09:41:54 +0800
大概模型会变成下面这样：`rt_gateway` 是 **NRT 内部的 RT 通讯代理服务**，上游面向 NRT 用 ROS2，下游面向 RT 用 `rt-sdk`。

```text
HMI
ProgramEngine
InstructionExecutionScheduler
DataDiagnostics
其他 NRT 模块
        |
        | ROS2 services / topics
        v
nrt/rt_gateway
        |
        | rt-sdk C++ API
        v
rt-sdk
        |
        | OPC UA backend / future backend
        v
RT RtApi
        |
        | Orocos services / ports
        v
MotionBank / ManualExecutor / AutomaticExecutor / WorkingModeManager
        |
        v
Core / HAL / simulation or EtherCAT
```

**模块分层**
```text
NRT user/application layer
  - nrt/hmi
  - nrt/program_engine
  - nrt/instruction_execution_scheduler
  - nrt/data_diagnostics
  - nrt/work_offset
  - nrt/tooling_management

NRT communication service layer
  - nrt/rt_gateway
    - owns RT connection lifecycle
    - owns ROS2 RT-facing service contract
    - owns local RT state cache
    - internally uses rt-sdk

SDK boundary
  - rt-sdk
    - Machine facade
    - backend abstraction
    - OPC UA backend

Shared contract
  - rt-api-contract
    - bounded wire structs
    - topology/status/command payloads
    - OPC UA datatype metadata

RT runtime
  - rt/Orocos/RtApi
  - MotionBank
  - ManualExecutor
  - AutomaticExecutor
  - WorkingModeManager
  - Core
  - HAL
```

**通信关系**
```text
NRT modules -> rt_gateway:
  ROS2 services:
    /rt_gateway/connect
    /rt_gateway/get_topology
    /rt_gateway/get_mode
    /rt_gateway/enter_manual
    /rt_gateway/enter_automatic
    /rt_gateway/begin_auto_session
    /rt_gateway/end_auto_session
    /rt_gateway/submit_auto_command
    /rt_gateway/manual_move
    /rt_gateway/servo_on
    /rt_gateway/enable
    ...

  ROS2 topics:
    /rt_gateway/connection_state
    /rt_gateway/topology
    /rt_gateway/mode_state
    /rt_gateway/axes_group_process_data
    /rt_gateway/spindle_process_data
    /rt_gateway/automatic_status
    /rt_gateway/manual_status
    /rt_gateway/events

rt_gateway -> rt-sdk:
  direct C++ calls:
    Machine::connect(config)
    machine.topology()
    machine.mode().enter_automatic()
    machine.automatic().begin_session()
    machine.automatic().queue().submit...
    machine.resources().axes_groups().servo_on(...)
    machine.resources().axes_groups().process_data()

rt-sdk -> RT:
  backend protocol:
    OPC UA now
    future backend later

RT -> rt_gateway:
  command receipts
  process data
  status snapshots
  topology/mode state
```

**关键职责边界**
- `rt_gateway` 持有 `rt-sdk::Machine`，其他 NRT 模块不直接持有。
- `rt_gateway` 负责连接、重连、endpoint 配置、backend 选择、RT 在线/离线状态。
- `rt_gateway` 把 RT SDK 的对象 API 转成 ROS2 services/topics。
- `rt_gateway` 不做 G-code 解析、不做运动规划、不拥有 WorkOffset/Tooling 真值。
- `ProgramEngine` 负责编程执行会话，不直接碰 OPC UA。
- `InstructionExecutionScheduler` 负责排序、阻塞、等待、提交策略，通过 `rt_gateway` 下发。
- `WorkOffset/Tooling` 仍是 NRT 域模型，给 ProgramEngine/Kernel/Scheduler 提供上下文，不直接和 RT 通讯。
- `rt-sdk` 仍是 userland 到 RT 的协议边界，不变成 ROS2 包的全局 API。
- `RT RtApi` 仍是 RT 侧 curated public surface，不暴露内部 `MotionBank` 作为外部 API。

**典型自动执行流程**
```text
HMI
  -> ProgramEngine.start_session(file, AUTO)

ProgramEngine
  -> parser 读取/解析程序
  -> NC Kernel 生成领域指令
  -> WorkOffset/Tooling 查询执行上下文
  -> Scheduler 排序和调度

Scheduler
  -> rt_gateway.enter_automatic()
  -> rt_gateway.begin_auto_session()
  -> rt_gateway.submit_auto_command(...)
  -> rt_gateway.cycle_start()
  -> 订阅 /rt_gateway/automatic_status 和 process_data
  -> 等待完成或错误
  -> rt_gateway.end_auto_session()

rt_gateway
  -> rt-sdk Machine API

rt-sdk
  -> OPC UA

RT RtApi
  -> AutomaticExecutor / MotionBank
```

**为什么这层合理**
- 多数 NRT 模块不需要知道 `rt-sdk`、OPC UA、endpoint、重连细节。
- ROS2 是 NRT 内部服务边界，适合 HMI、ProgramEngine、Diagnostics 共享。
- `rt-sdk` 保持低层、稳定、可被测试和外部工具直接使用。
- RT 侧继续只维护 `RtApi`，不被 NRT 模块各自绑定一遍。
- 后续从 OPC UA 换 backend，只改 `rt_gateway` 配置和 `rt-sdk` backend，不扩散到 NRT 业务模块。

**需要新增的东西**
```text
nrt/rt_gateway/
  package.xml
  CMakeLists.txt
  include/...
  src/
    rt_gateway_node.cpp
    rt_gateway_core.cpp
    rt_sdk_client_adapter.cpp
    state_cache.cpp
  srv/
    Connect.srv
    GetTopology.srv
    EnterMode.srv
    BeginSession.srv
    EndSession.srv
    SubmitAutomaticCommand.srv
    ManualMove.srv
    ResourceCommand.srv
  msg/
    ConnectionState.msg
    MachineTopology.msg
    ModeState.msg
    AxesGroupProcessData.msg
    SpindleProcessData.msg
    AutomaticStatus.msg
    CommandReceipt.msg
```

一句话：加入 `rt_gateway` 后，MetaNC 的 NRT/RT 关系会从“理论上 NRT 直接通过 rt-sdk 到 RT”变成更干净的 **NRT 模块通过 ROS2 调用 rt_gateway，rt_gateway 独占使用 rt-sdk 与 RT 通讯**。这就是现在架构里缺的中间服务层。

Codex Response 13 / commentary

2026-06-24 10:00:09 +0800

Codex Response 15 / commentary

2026-06-24 10:02:00 +0800

Codex Response 16 / commentary

2026-06-24 10:02:45 +0800
我还会引用 `PARAMETER-DESCRIPTORS` 和 `PARAMETER-INSTANCE-BINDINGS` 的行号：它们说明 descriptor 只定义参数身份，不是实时值、不带当前值；instance binding 只解决“这个参数实例绑定到哪个机床对象”。这正是把参数设计接入总体架构时最容易混淆的点。

Codex Response 17 / final_answer

2026-06-24 10:05:29 +0800
结论：前面只谈 `rt_gateway` 还不算“结合了所有部分”。结合 `/tmp/abc` 三张会图、MetaNC 当前代码和 `metanc_drafts/TP004` 后，完整模型应该把 **Program Execution、Parameter/Runtime Access、RT Gateway** 三条主线一起画出来。参数这块要纳入，但不能把它简单当成“全局参数表”或放进 `rt_gateway` 里。

**总体模型**

```text
HMI / HMI Backend
  |  ROS2 services/topics, current HMI server adapters
  v
NRT Application Layer
  |-- ProgramEngine / Execution Policy
  |-- Parameter System + Runtime Access
  |-- Data & Diagnostics
  |
  |-- Tooling Management      <- domain owner
  |-- Work Offset             <- domain owner
  |-- Machine Configuration   <- axis/channel/frame/source lists
  |
  v
RT Gateway
  |  NRT 唯一产品级 rt-sdk 持有者
  v
rt-sdk
  |  OPC UA backend, SDK public contract
  v
rt-api-contract / RT RtApi
  |
  v
RT Kernel / RT PLC / RT HALs / RT Fieldbus / RT OS
  |
  v
Device Simulations / EtherCAT devices
```

**参数放在哪**

参数应该是 `nrt/parameter_system` 或 `nrt/runtime_access` 这一层的核心能力，不是 `rt_gateway` 的子模块。TP004 明确说它覆盖的是“参数与运行时访问模型”，包括 governed parameters、tool/zero-offset 领域表、HAL-like signals、runtime state、PLC/NC/program symbols 等，而不是单一参数表。[TP004 README](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/metanc_drafts/TP004-parameter-system/openspec/README.md:13)

所以参数层应负责：

- `Unified Access Registry`：统一查找、别名、访问检查、owner 分发；不是一个存储后端。[DESIGN-BASELINE.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/metanc_drafts/TP004-parameter-system/openspec/DESIGN-BASELINE.md:74)
- `Parameter Catalog / Descriptor`：只描述 governed parameter 的身份、owner、scope、semantic_name、value metadata；不是实时值、当前值、UI path 或 840D 原生命名。[PARAMETER-DESCRIPTORS.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/metanc_drafts/TP004-parameter-system/openspec/PARAMETER-DESCRIPTORS.md:14)
- `Parameter Instance Binding`：把一个 descriptor 绑定到具体机床对象实例，比如 axis_slot、G54/X/TR；不定义运行值、访问行为、生效时机。[PARAMETER-INSTANCE-BINDINGS.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/metanc_drafts/TP004-parameter-system/openspec/PARAMETER-INSTANCE-BINDINGS.md:3)
- `Binding Resolver`：NC/PLC/program symbol 预解析到参数、signal、runtime state、domain-table operation 或 fast slot；确定性路径不能运行时字符串查找。[TERMS.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/metanc_drafts/TP004-parameter-system/openspec/TERMS.md:222)

**rt_gateway 的位置**

`rt_gateway` 是 NRT 到 RT 的通讯聚合层。它应该拥有 `rt-sdk::Machine` 连接、连接状态、RT 状态缓存、命令转换和 ROS2 服务/Topic，不应该拥有参数目录或领域数据。

产品级规则应是：

- HMI、ProgramEngine、Parameters、Diagnostics 等 NRT 模块不直接用 `rt-sdk`。
- 它们通过 ROS2 service/topic 调 `rt_gateway`。
- `rt_gateway -> rt-sdk -> OPC UA -> RT RtApi`。
- RT 内部仍通过 RtApi/Backends 映射到 Core/Orocos，不暴露 MotionBank 等内部实现。

这和当前 repo 边界一致：`rt-sdk` 是 userland RT 通信边界，[rt-sdk README](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt-sdk/README.md:1)；`rt-api-contract` 是 RT 与 rt-sdk 共享 wire contract，且 rt-sdk 不允许链接 RT Core/Orocos。[rt-api-contract README](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt-api-contract/README.md:6)

**关键交互**

- HMI 读/改参数：`HMI -> Parameter Service -> Unified Access Registry -> owner dispatch`。如果是刀具表，分发到 Tooling；如果是零偏，分发到 WorkOffset；如果是 RT-bound motion/hal/runtime state，则通过 `rt_gateway` 读快照或申请 apply。
- 程序执行：`HMI -> ProgramEngine -> Parser -> NC Kernel -> Scheduler -> rt_gateway -> rt-sdk -> RT`。ProgramEngine 文档已经定义公共 seam 是 ROS2，[MODULE-MODEL.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/nrt/program_engine/docs/spec/MODULE-MODEL.md:95)，并接受 ROS2 services + lifecycle topic 的模型。[0013 decision](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/nrt/program_engine/docs/spec/MODULE-MODEL.decision/0013-ros2-seam-transport-agnostic-core.md:31)
- 参数应用到 RT：`Parameter Service` 先做 owner/access/activation 校验，再经 `Execution Policy` 判断当前模式和运行状态，最后由 `rt_gateway` 调 RT SDK。RT 参数 API 需要进入 `rt-api-contract/RtApi`，否则只能停留在 NRT 配置或 deploy-time 配置。
- 诊断数据：`rt_gateway` 发布 RT 状态、执行状态、过程数据；`Parameter Service` 发布参数变更/audit；ProgramEngine 发布 session/lifecycle；Data & Diagnostics 汇总。
- 仿真验证：Devices-Simulations 应走同一条 `rt_gateway -> rt-sdk -> RT RtApi` 路径，不要另开一套测试私有接口；西门子运行逻辑验证则同时覆盖 HMI 功能可见性、模式策略、参数/刀具/零偏访问。

**当前缺口**

当前仓库已有 `rt-sdk`、`rt-api-contract`、RT `RtApi`/OPC UA 暴露，以及 HMI 对 Tooling/WorkOffset 的同进程集成。[xyzac-spindle.ops](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt/Orocos/deploy/xyzac_spindle_sim/xyzac-spindle.ops:129) [HMI CMakeLists](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/nrt/hmi/server/CMakeLists.txt:84)

但还缺这些架构层：

- `nrt/rt_gateway` 实现和 ROS2 `.srv/.msg` 接口。
- `nrt/parameter_system` / runtime access service 实现。
- 参数 apply/read/protect 的访问契约、事件、审计、pending/applied/effective 状态。
- RT-bound 参数进入 `rt-api-contract` 和 RT `RtApi` 的 contract。
- `Execution Policy` 独立边界，至少要被 HMI Backend、ProgramEngine、Parameter Service、RT Gateway 共用。
- Data & Diagnostics 后端服务层。
- ProgramEngine/Scheduler 的实际 ROS2 adapter 与 RT Gateway 连接。

我的建议是：下一版架构图里不要只加 `RT-Gateway`，而是同时新增 `Parameter System / Runtime Access` 和 `Execution Policy`。否则会解决“谁连 RT”的问题，但仍解决不了会议图里 Parameters 那条核心诉求：参数如何读、改、保护、传播、应用，以及如何避免每个模块重复处理参数逻辑。

Codex Response 2 / final_answer

2026-06-24 10:11:25 +0800
先给结论：**大方向合理，但现在不能只补 `rt_gateway`。应该同时明确三层：`rt_gateway`、`parameter_system/runtime_access`、`execution_policy`。** 目前仓库里 RT 底座和部分 NRT 领域模块已有，NRT 服务层和参数运行时层缺得比较明显。

**已有且合理**

| 能力 | 现状 | 判断 |
|---|---|---|
| `rt-sdk` + OPC UA backend | 已有，定位是 userland 到 RT 的 SDK 边界 | 合理，保留，不让普通 NRT 模块直接散用 |
| `rt-api-contract` | 已有，定义 RT/SDK 共享 wire contract，禁止 SDK 链接 RT Core/Orocos | 合理，是 RT 边界的正确做法 |
| RT `RtApi` | 已有，当前部署暴露 `system/mode/topology/resources/manual/automatic/mda` | 合理，是 RT 内部到 SDK 的公共门面 |
| HMI + Tooling + WorkOffset | 已有同进程集成，HMI server 可直接链接 tooling/work_offset | 合理作为当前过渡实现，但不能推广成所有 NRT 通信模型 |
| Tooling / WorkOffset 领域模块 | 已有代码和文档 | 合理，应该继续作为领域 truth owner，而不是被参数模块吞掉 |
| TP004 参数设计文档 | 在 `metanc_drafts` 已有 active baseline | 方向合理：参数是 broader runtime access model，不是扁平参数表 |

相关边界：`rt-sdk` README 明确它是 userland SDK；[`rt-api-contract`](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/rt-api-contract/README.md:6) 明确共享 contract 边界；TP004 明确参数系统覆盖参数、领域表、信号、运行状态、PLC/NC/program symbols 等访问模型。[TP004 README](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/metanc_drafts/TP004-parameter-system/openspec/README.md:13)

**缺省且应补**

| 缺口 | 当前状态 | 建议 |
|---|---|---|
| `nrt/rt_gateway` | 目录不存在 | 近期应推进 |
| ROS2 `.srv/.msg/.action` | 我复核 `rg --files -g '*.srv' -g '*.msg' -g '*.action'`，当前无输出 | 需要新建接口层 |
| `nrt/parameter_system` / runtime access | MetaNC 主仓无实现，只有 `metanc_drafts` 设计 | 先做窄切片，不做大而全 |
| `execution_policy` | 会议图提出，但仓库无独立模块 | 应抽成共享策略层，不能只放 HMI Backend |
| `nrt/data_diagnostics` | architecture 中有模块名，但目录不存在 | 先补事件/audit，不急着做完整数据中心 |
| `nrt/nc_kernel` / `instruction_execution_scheduler` / `motion_algorithms` | architecture 里有，目录不存在 | 随 ProgramEngine 推进，不要先空转大框架 |
| ProgramEngine 运行实现 | 目前主要是 docs/spec，未见 ROS2 package/实现目录 | 可作为下一阶段主线，但先接通 gateway 更实际 |

**需要调整的点**

- `architecture.md` 现在写的是 scheduler 直接 dispatch through `/rt-sdk`，如果接受 `rt_gateway`，这条应改成 `scheduler -> rt_gateway -> rt-sdk`。[architecture.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/MetaNC/docs/dev/architecture.md:226)
- “HMI-Backend 需要 Execution Policy”这个说法太窄。Execution Policy 应被 HMI、ProgramEngine、Parameter Service、RT Gateway 共用，HMI 只是消费者。
- Parameters 不能变成每个模块一套 `set_xxx` 的包装层。TP004 已经说明 `Parameter Descriptor` 不是实时值、不是当前值、不是 UI path，也不是 controller-native expression。[PARAMETER-DESCRIPTORS.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/metanc_drafts/TP004-parameter-system/openspec/PARAMETER-DESCRIPTORS.md:14)
- 参数系统也不应该吞掉 Tooling/WorkOffset。Tool table、zero offsets 是 domain tables，应 dispatch 到 owner API。[DESIGN-BASELINE.md](/home/iaar/workspace/ccmix-wp/metanc_drafts/TP004-parameter-system/openspec/DESIGN-BASELINE.md:119)
- `rt_gateway` 不应拥有参数目录。它只负责 RT 通讯、状态缓存、命令转发、RT-bound apply。

**可立即推进**

1. 新增 `nrt/rt_gateway` 的最小切片：连接 RT、读 topology/mode/status、发布 gateway health，内部唯一持有 `rt-sdk::Machine`。
2. 新增 ROS2 interface 包或 gateway-local interfaces：先做 `Connect/GetTopology/GetMode/SetMode/GetStatus`，不要一上来覆盖所有自动执行。
3. 架构文档调整：把 `rt_gateway` 插在所有 NRT 与 `rt-sdk` 之间。
4. 参数系统做窄切片：`Descriptor Catalog + Instance Binding + owner dispatch`，先接 WorkOffset/Tooling 的读路径和少量写路径。
5. Execution Policy 做最小版：模式、运行中/停止中、手动/自动/MDI 下哪些命令允许。
6. Data/Diagnostics 先接事件流：gateway 状态、参数变更 audit、ProgramEngine lifecycle。

**可以搁置**

- 完整 Siemens 840D 参数目录覆盖。
- RT live 参数全量写入，尤其运动学、伺服参数、fieldbus 下载。
- 完整 PLC 语言/buildtime/runtime 全链路。
- 完整数据中心、时间同步、长期存储分析。
- 24h 稳定性指标的正式验收。先有 gateway 和压力脚本，再谈这个。
- 多通道复杂同步、完整换刀 PLC 序列、两套刀库最终结构。

建议优先顺序是：**先补 `rt_gateway` 最小闭环，再补参数系统窄切片，再让 ProgramEngine/Scheduler 接 gateway。** 这样最短路径能把 RT Docker/rt-sdk/HMI/NRT 模块串起来，同时不会把还在讨论中的参数整体设计过早写死。

Codex Response 1 / final_answer

2026-06-24 09:24:44 +0800
会，有一定误导风险。不是大问题，但我建议不要把 `R1/U1/C1/C2` 继续当主命名。

主要风险：

- `R1` 容易被读成 release、runtime phase，甚至和 Siemens `R` 参数联想混在一起。
- `U1` 太短，别人不一定能立刻看出是 Unit governance。
- `C1/C2` 会让人以为已经进入一套新的正式 C-series 计划。
- TP004 已经有 `TP004`、`P3-P9`、OpenSpec change、discussion pack 多套编号，再加一套字母编号会增加认知负担。
- 当前文档里它们只是“后续 slice 示例名”，但看起来像正式路线图编号。

我建议改成描述性名称，不用短编号：

- `runtime/configuration active-profile handoff`
- `concrete unit governance`
- `constraint metadata shape`
- `constraint checker validation after metadata shape`

如果确实需要编号，最好用明确前缀，例如：

- `TP004-FU-runtime-profile-handoff`
- `TP004-FU-unit-governance`
- `TP004-FU-constraint-shape`
- `TP004-FU-constraint-checker`

我的判断：**R/U/C 这组命名不值得保留为正式标签；最多作为一次性口头简称，文档里最好改成描述性 slice 名称。**

1. **短期：不动作，只观察**
   - 当前最稳。
   - 适合先把 TP004 阶段状态用于后续 MetaNC/HMI/runtime 讨论。

   - 明确 active machine profile 谁选择、谁加载。

   - 讨论 `mm / deg / mm/min` 这类具体单位归谁管。
   - 不碰 runtime conversion。
   - 不改 `value.unit_source` 当前语义，除非单独 proposal 明确接受。

4. **如果要做约束：先开 `constraint metadata shape proposal`**
   - 只决定 `default/min/max/step/precision` 是字段还是结构化 block。
   - 不做 checker，不做 runtime enforcement。

5. **最后才是 `constraint checker validation`**
   - 只有 constraint metadata shape 已接受后再做。
   - checker 只校验 metadata shape，不代表运行时写入校验。

我的建议：**下一步先别开新 TP004 change；等你确定要接 runtime/profile 还是 unit/constraint，再开一个描述性 slice。**当前最自然的后续是把 TP004 作为阶段基线转到 MetaNC/runtime 集成讨论。

- `02-module-status.png` 可用，但 `nrt/parameter_system` 现在还只是候选落地方向，drafts 里建议写成 `Parameter System / Runtime Access narrow slice`，避免过早承诺目录名。
- `05-adjustments.png` 适合表达“需要避免的误解”，但不建议直接叫“当前说法”；比如 `Scheduler 直接调 rt-sdk` 更像风险/误区，不一定是当前已发生事实。
- `06-roadmap.png` 顺序合理，不过图里的 `P1/P2/P3...` 容易和 TP004 历史 P 编号混淆，正式 drafts 里建议改成 `Stage 1/2/3` 或描述性 milestone。

和当前 `MetaNC` 对照后，事实边界是：

- 当前 `MetaNC` 在 `main...origin/main`，工作区干净。
- 当前 `nrt` 一级目录只有 `dev / gcode_parser / gcode_parser_test / hmi / program_engine / tooling_management / work_offset`。
- 还没有 `nrt/rt_gateway`、`nrt/parameter_system`、`runtime_access` 这类实现目录。
- 所以这批图应归类为：**下一阶段架构规划图 + drafts 输入材料**。

我建议下一步等你确认图中文字是否还要微调后，再落一个总架构 discussion pack，标题可以是：

`2026-06-24 MetaNC NRT/RT Gateway Architecture Roadmap`

你结合这些图和之前的内容给我一个总结

Codex Response 1 / commentary

2026-06-24 15:45:35 +0800

Codex Response 2 / final_answer

2026-06-24 15:52:42 +0800
综合这些图和前面 TP004、WorkOffset、MetaNC 现状，我的总结是：

**核心判断**

MetaNC 下一阶段最关键的不是继续扩 TP004，而是补一条清晰的 **NRT -> RT 主链路**。这条主链路的中心是 `nrt/rt_gateway`：普通 NRT 模块不直接使用 `rt-sdk`，而是通过 gateway 进入 RT。

当前架构可以这样理解：

```text
HMI / ProgramEngine / Parameter Runtime Access / Diagnostics
        |
        v
Execution Policy
        |
        v
nrt/rt_gateway
        |
        v
rt-sdk + rt-api-contract
        |
        v
RT RtApi / RT Kernel / PLC / HAL / Fieldbus / OS
```

**已经比较确定的部分**

- `rt-sdk` 和 `rt-api-contract` 已经是可依赖基础。
- RT 侧 `RtApi` 已存在。
- Tooling / WorkOffset 是 NRT 领域 owner，不应该被参数系统吞掉。
- WorkOffset V1 证明了“领域模块 + HMI adapter”的方式可行，但它不能直接推广成所有 RT 通信模式。
- TP004 当前已完成的是 descriptor / zero-offset governance 阶段基线，不是完整参数运行时访问系统。

**当前缺口**

- 缺 `nrt/rt_gateway`：NRT 侧统一 RT 通讯入口。
- 缺共享 `Execution Policy`：模式、运行态、权限、互锁、activation timing 不应只散落在 HMI backend。
- 缺 Parameter System / Runtime Access 的运行时窄切片：现在 TP004 有描述符治理，但还没有真正的 runtime access dispatch 闭环。
- 缺 Diagnostics/Data 的最小事件面：gateway、参数变更、执行状态、RT 状态需要能进入统一诊断/审计。
- 缺仿真主链路：仿真不应另开私有路径，而应尽量复用 gateway/rt-sdk/RtApi 主链路。

**参数系统的边界**

参数系统不应被设计成一个大而全的全局参数表。更合理的是：

```text
外部入口
  HMI 参数页 / NC-PLC 符号 / Siemens-style $ 视图
        |
        v
Unified Access Registry
        |
        v
Binding Resolver
        |
        +-- Parameter Catalog
        +-- Domain Tables: Tooling / WorkOffset / Magazine
        +-- Signal / HAL
        +-- Runtime State
        +-- Runtime Symbols
```

也就是说，TP004 后续应只做 **runtime access / owner dispatch / binding resolver** 这条窄线，不要回到大规模参数目录或完整 840D catalog。

**7.30 前建议收敛成 5 个 drafts 交付**

- 总架构 pack：把这批图和当前代码事实合成一份架构路线说明。
- RT Gateway baseline：定义 gateway 职责、非职责、ROS2 service/topic 或等价接口、rt-sdk 持有边界。
- Parameter Runtime Access follow-up：从 TP004 延伸，但只做 descriptor -> instance binding -> owner dispatch 的窄切片。
- Execution Policy baseline：定义 mode guard、running/paused/faulted guard、参数 activation timing、HMI/ProgramEngine/gateway 共用边界。
- HMI/Simulation validation matrix：列出 7.31 前 UI 功能验证项，比如模式支持、MDI、程序执行、换刀、错误恢复、诊断界面、仿真路径。

**需要避免的误导**

- 不要说 `rt_gateway` 已实现；现在它是缺失关键层。
- 不要把 `Parameter Service` 写成已存在模块；它目前更像候选抽象。
- 不要把 TP004 close 理解成参数系统完成；只是 descriptor 阶段完成。
- 不要把 Tooling / WorkOffset 纳入参数层所有权；它们仍是领域 owner。
- 不要继续用 P1/P2/P3 这种编号，容易和 TP004 历史 P 编号混淆；后续建议用描述性 milestone。

一句话：**当前图包已经把下一阶段路线收敛清楚了：先补 RT Gateway 主链路，再接参数运行时访问，再抽共享执行策略，最后让 ProgramEngine、Diagnostics、Simulation 都走同一条主链路。**

Turn 18

User

2026-06-24 15:53:53 +0800
你给我一个通俗的描述，关于我们的TODO

Codex Response 1 / final_answer

2026-06-24 16:01:17 +0800
通俗讲，我们现在的 TODO 是把 MetaNC 从“几个模块各自能跑”整理成“一条清楚的系统主干”。

现在已经有的东西：

- RT 底层有能力，`rt-sdk`、`rt-api-contract`、`RtApi` 都已经有基础。
- HMI、Tooling、WorkOffset 也已经做出了一些真实集成。
- TP004 参数描述符这一阶段已经收口，知道“参数应该怎么描述、怎么绑定到对象”。

但现在缺的是“中间总线”和“统一规矩”。

接下来 TODO 可以理解成 5 件事：

- **先建 RT Gateway**
  让所有 NRT 上层模块不要各自直接找 `rt-sdk`，而是统一通过一个 `rt_gateway` 跟 RT 说话。它负责连接、模式、状态、拓扑、执行请求这些基础通信。

- **整理参数访问入口**
  参数不是一张大表。刀具、零偏、机床配置、HAL 信号、运行状态都有自己的 owner。我们要做的是统一“怎么查、怎么写、怎么保护、怎么分发给 owner”。

- **抽出执行策略**
  什么模式下能点什么按钮，运行中能不能改参数，暂停/故障/自动模式下哪些命令要拒绝，这些规则不能散在 HMI 里，要变成共享的 Execution Policy。

- **让 ProgramEngine 接进主链路**
  程序执行、MDI、暂停、恢复、停止，后面都应该通过 `ProgramEngine -> RT Gateway -> RT`，而不是各模块绕路。

- **把诊断和仿真接到同一路径**
  日志、事件、状态变化、参数变更、RT 状态都要进诊断面；仿真也尽量走真实主链路，不搞一套只给 demo 用的私有通道。

最短一句话：

**我们下一步不是继续堆模块，而是先把“上层怎么安全、统一地访问 RT 和参数”的主干搭起来。**

| Execution Policy | 缺失共享层 | 低 | 规则还没抽成 HMI/ProgramEngine/参数/gateway 共用策略 |
| ProgramEngine -> RT | 未接主链路 | 低中 | ProgramEngine 有方向，但还没通过 gateway 进入 RT |
| Data / Diagnostics | 未形成统一面 | 低 | 日志、事件、状态、审计还没统一接起来 |
| Simulation 主链路 | 未收敛 | 低 | 仿真应走 gateway/rt-sdk/RtApi 主链路，目前还需规划 |

通信主链路当前状态：

| 通信链路 | 当前状态 |
|---|---|
| HMI -> HMI Backend | 已有 |
| HMI Backend -> Tooling / WorkOffset | 已有过渡集成 |
| NRT 模块 -> `rt-sdk` | 不应直接做 |
| NRT 模块 -> `rt_gateway` -> `rt-sdk` -> RT | 目标链路，未完成 |
| ProgramEngine -> RT | 未完成主链路 |
| Parameter Runtime Access -> owner / RT | 未完成 |
| RT 状态 -> Diagnostics | 未完成统一链路 |

一句话：**现在是 RT、HMI、Tooling、WorkOffset 有基础；缺的是 `rt_gateway`、共享执行策略、参数运行时访问、统一诊断这条系统主干。**

   - 配置系统：推进 TP005。
   - Execution Policy：先作为跨模块策略，不急着独立 TP。

一句话：**下一步先 discussion 定边界；第一个真正适合新建的 TP 是 `RT Gateway`。**

2026-06-24 09:07:29 +0800

主要占用按优先级：

- `~/workspace`：`9.5G`，最大的是 `ccmix-wp 7.1G`、`github 2.4G`。
- `~/.npm`：`2.3G`，主要是 `_cacache 1.5G` 和 `_npx 776M`。这是最典型可清缓存。
- `~/.vscode-server`：`2.0G`，扩展 `1.2G`，server bin `485M`，缓存 VSIX `170M`。可以清旧扩展/缓存，但别直接全删，除非愿意让 VS Code 重新安装。
- `~/.cache`：`2.0G`，大头是浏览器自动化缓存：`puppeteer 636M`、`ms-playwright 631M`、`hyperframes 243M`、`uv 296M`。这些基本可重建，但删了下次会重新下载。
- `~/.local`：`1.9G`，主要是 `~/.local/share/claude/versions 1.4G`，这是 Claude Code 本体版本缓存/安装，不建议盲删当前版本。
- `~/.codex`：`1.6G`，不是 session 大了，而是 `logs_2.sqlite` 约 `1.44G`；session 现在约 `91M`。这个日志库是最大异常点。
- `~/.rustup`：`1.4G`，一个 Rust stable toolchain；不建议删，除非不用 Rust。
- `~/.nvm`：`1.1G`，单个 Node `v24.14.1`，其中 `lib 846M`；这是环境安装，不是缓存。
- `~/.cargo`：`487M`，registry `397M`，可清但会影响 Rust 构建速度。
- `~/.claude`：只有 `11M`，session 很小，不是问题。

我建议的清理优先级：

1. 低风险可清：`~/.npm/_npx`、`~/.npm/_cacache`、`~/.cache/pip`、`~/.cache/node-gyp`、`~/.cache/vcpkg`。
2. 中等风险可重下：`~/.cache/puppeteer`、`~/.cache/ms-playwright`、`~/.cache/hyperframes`，会导致下次跑浏览器测试/视频工具重新下载几百 MB。
3. 需要先确认：`~/.codex/logs_2.sqlite`，这个单文件 `1.44G`，最值得处理，但要确认 Codex 当前是否依赖它，最好在没有 Codex 活跃会话时做压缩/归档/删除。
4. 不建议直接删：`~/.local/share/claude/versions`、`~/.nvm/versions/node`、`~/.rustup/toolchains`、`~/.vscode-server/extensions`，这些是工具安装或开发环境。

Turn 5

User

2026-06-24 10:00:23 +0800
- ~/.npm/_npx：776M
  - ~/.npm/_cacache：1.5G 这几个是什么缓存

Codex Response 1 / final_answer

2026-06-24 10:00:53 +0800
这是 npm 的两类本地缓存。

`~/.npm/_cacache` 是 npm 的内容缓存。你执行 `npm install`、`npm exec`、`npx` 时下载过的 package tarball、metadata、索引等会放这里。作用是下次安装同一个包时少联网、速度更快。删了通常没问题，npm 会按需重新下载。标准清法是：

```bash
npm cache clean --force
```

`~/.npm/_npx` 是 `npx` / `npm exec` 临时安装过的可执行包工作目录。比如临时跑过 `npx hyperframes`、`npx playwright`、`npx some-cli`，npm 会把那次执行需要的包放到 `_npx/<hash>/`。这里更像“一次性工具运行缓存”。删了也通常没问题，下次再 `npx ...` 会重新装。

你这边最大的是：

```text
~/.npm/_cacache  1.5G
~/.npm/_npx      776M
```

判断：这两块都属于可重建缓存，可以清。代价是下次 npm/npx 首次运行会慢，并且需要网络。

Codex Response 5 / final_answer

2026-06-24 11:25:06 +0800
结论：不要一把 `docker system prune -a`。这几张图是 MetaNC 的一组构建/运行镜像，只有一个 dangling 旧图可以直接删。

| 镜像 | Unique 占用 | 用途 | 当前引用 | 建议 |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `optimalcnc/metanc:latest` | `3.88GB` | MetaNC 大型 build/dev base，含 Orocos/Rock；HMI/RT runtime Dockerfile 都拿它做 builder | README 说明用于 local Docker/devcontainer/CI；`rt-runtime/Dockerfile` 默认 builder 是它 | **保留**。删了以后构建 RT runtime/HMI Docker 会重新拉/重建，很慢 |
| `metanc-hmi-server-build:local` | `5.8GB` | HMI server 的 Docker 构建阶段缓存镜像，用于 `HMI_SERVER_NATIVE_BUILD_MODE=docker` 时生成 native server | `generate_targets.sh` 默认 `HMI_SERVER_DOCKER_BUILDER_IMAGE=metanc-hmi-server-build:local` | **可确认后删**。如果你主要走 host build，短期可删；下次强制 docker build 会重建约 6-7GB |
| `metanc-hmi-server:local` | `63MB` | HMI server 运行镜像，docker compose 启动/ smoke 用 | `docker_hmi_server.sh` 和 `compose.hmi-server.yml` 默认使用 | **保留**。很小，且是运行入口 |
| `optimalcnc/metanc-rt:latest` | `701MB` | RT simulation runtime，给 RT SDK / NRT 开 ready OPC UA endpoint，不用本地编译 RT | README、RT docs、SDK quick-start 都直接引用 | **保留**。不大，而且用途明确 |
| `<none>:<none>` `47c0de91df02` | `675MB` | 旧的 dangling image；从创建时间和大小看像旧版 `optimalcnc/metanc-rt` 被新 tag 覆盖后留下的旧层 | 无 tag、无容器引用 | **可删**。这是最明确的清理项 |

当前 Docker 状态：

| 项 | 状态 |
|---|---:|
| 运行/停止容器 | `0` |
| Docker volume | `0` |
| BuildKit cache | `0B` |
| 可直接确认删除项 | dangling `<none>`，约 `675MB` |
| 可选择删除项 | `metanc-hmi-server-build:local`，约 `5.8GB unique`，代价是下次 Docker-native HMI build 要重建 |

我的建议：

```text
现在只删 <none>:<none> 是安全的。
如果你确定近期不跑 HMI_SERVER_NATIVE_BUILD_MODE=docker，再删 metanc-hmi-server-build:local。
其他三个保留。
```

对应命令如果要执行：

```bash
docker rmi 47c0de91df02

```

第二条我建议先不动，除非你明确要换空间。前三个 tagged runtime/base 镜像不建议删。

Turn 11

User

2026-06-24 13:07:55 +0800
看一下MetaNC里哪些hmi的选项会触发这个重新构建

Codex Response 1 / commentary

2026-06-24 13:08:28 +0800
我会直接查 MetaNC HMI 的脚本和文档里哪些参数会走 Docker-native build，区分“重建 `metanc-hmi-server-build:local`”和“重建 HMI runtime 镜像”。

Codex Response 2 / commentary

2026-06-24 13:09:06 +0800
我再补一个当前环境判断：`auto` 模式到底会不会在你这个 shell 里走 Docker，取决于 `VCPKG_ROOT` 或显式 CMake toolchain 是否存在。

Codex Response 3 / final_answer

2026-06-24 13:09:25 +0800
会触发 `metanc-hmi-server-build:local` 重建的只有 HMI 的 **native server 生成走 Docker path** 这一类，不是所有 HMI 运行都会触发。

| 操作/选项 | 会不会重建 `metanc-hmi-server-build:local` | 说明 |
|---|---:|---|
| `cd nrt/hmi && ./tools/generate_targets.sh` | 当前环境下 **不会** | 你当前 `VCPKG_ROOT=/home/iaar/workspace/github/vcpkg` 存在，`auto` 会走 host build |
| `HMI_SERVER_NATIVE_BUILD_MODE=host ./tools/generate_targets.sh` | 不会 | 强制 host build |
| `HMI_SERVER_NATIVE_BUILD_MODE=docker ./tools/generate_targets.sh` | **会** | 明确触发 `docker build --target build -t metanc-hmi-server-build:local` |
| 未设置 `VCPKG_ROOT` 且无 `HMI_SERVER_CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE` 时跑 `./tools/generate_targets.sh` | **会** | `auto` 检测不到 host toolchain，会 fallback 到 docker |
| 设置 `HMI_SERVER_DOCKER_BUILDER_IMAGE=xxx` 并走 docker mode | 会，但 tag 变成 `xxx` | 默认 tag 才是 `metanc-hmi-server-build:local` |

另外这几类会重建的是 **另一个小 runtime 镜像** `metanc-hmi-server:local`，不是 builder 缓存镜像：

| 操作 | 重建哪个镜像 |
|---|---|
| `./tools/docker_hmi_server.sh build` | `metanc-hmi-server:local` |
| `./tools/docker_hmi_server.sh run` | `metanc-hmi-server:local`，因为内部 `compose up --build` |
| `./tools/docker_hmi_server.sh smoke` | `metanc-hmi-server:local`，因为内部 `compose up -d --build` |

`MetaNC-RealTime` working tree and keeps the portable CNC core plus platform

# RT SDK

The RT SDK is the userland interface for connecting to a realtime control
application and observing and commanding the machine-control surface it exposes.

## What You Connect To

- One SDK connection targets one RT application endpoint.
- In v1, that RT application exposes one machine-control surface, represented by
  `Machine`.
- `Machine` is the user-facing CNC operation handle: topology, resources, modes,
  manual / automatic / MDA control, parameters, and status.

> [!NOTE]
> `Machine` is the machine-control surface, not the RT endpoint itself. In a
> future version one RT application may expose multiple machine-control
> surfaces; a higher-level object (for example `RtApplication` or `Controller`
> with `application.machine(id)`) would be added then. The v1 model is one
> connection → one RT application → one `Machine`.

The SDK keeps application code on a stable public contract while backend code
handles transport to the RT application.

Most clients call `connect(config)` to get one `Machine`, then use that
machine for discovery, coordination, control workflows, and resource state.
This diagram shows that top-level path.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    App["User application"]
    Backend["SDK Communication Backend<br/>target-specific transport"]
    Runtime["Realtime application"]
    Connect["Machine Connection API<br/>connect(config)"]
    Machine["Machine"]
    Queries["Queries<br/>topology / control_status"]
    Resources["Resources<br/>servo power / process data"]
    Parameters["Parameters<br/>deferred"]
    Logs["Logs<br/>deferred"]
    Control["Control<br/>mode / manual / auto"]
    Catalog["Tool Catalog<br/>deferred"]

    App --> Connect
    Connect --> Backend
    Backend --> Runtime
    Connect --> Machine
    Machine --> Queries
    Machine --> Resources
    Machine --> Parameters
    Machine --> Logs
    Machine --> Control
    Machine --> Catalog
```

| Need | Page |
|---|---|
| run a ready RT endpoint and prepare SDK dependencies | [Prerequisites](./prerequisites.md) |
| understand the SDK in one short flow | [Quick Start](./quick-start.md) |
| understand object layout, ownership, interlocks, and topology rules | [Concepts](./concepts/index.md) |
| review or run RT/SDK user stories and expected results | [Contracts](./contracts/index.md) |
| find exact C++ symbols | [API Reference](./api-reference.md) |

# Quick Start

## Build The SDK

```bash
cd rt-sdk
cmake --preset dev
cmake --build --preset dev
cmake --build --preset quick-start
```

## Run RT Side

Check [Prerequisites](./prerequisites.md) first. The SDK example needs a
running RT runtime at `opc.tcp://localhost:4840`.

Start the packaged simulation runtime in terminal 1:

{{#tabs }}
{{#tab name="Docker" }}
```bash
docker run --rm -it -p 4840:4840 optimalcnc/metanc-rt:latest
```
{{#endtab }}
{{#tab name="Podman" }}
```bash
podman run --rm -it -p 4840:4840 docker.io/optimalcnc/metanc-rt:latest
```
{{#endtab }}
{{#endtabs }}

Keep this container running.

If you are changing RT source code instead of only using the SDK, run the local
deployer path from [RT Run Scripts](/rt/docs/getting-started/run-scripts.md)
instead.

## Run SDK Side

Run the SDK example from terminal 2 at the repository root:

```bash
./rt-sdk/build/quick-start/meta_nc_rt_sdk_quick_start_manual_axes_group \
  --endpoint opc.tcp://localhost:4840
```

## Flow

The program does the following:

1. Connect to `opc.tcp://localhost:4840`.
2. Verify the runtime exposes the reviewed `XYZAC + SP0` topology.
3. Start the axes-group process-data monitor.
4. Servo-on and enable axes group `0`.
5. Enter manual mode.
6. Read the current axes-group positions.
7. Build a target by adding `{100, 80, 60, 1, 1}` in `X, Y, Z, A, C` order.
8. Submit one synchronized MCS absolute move with a `50.0` machine-unit/s
   command velocity cap.
9. Poll axes-group process data until the state machine leaves and returns to
   standstill, then verify final positions against the target.
10. Stop axes group `0` as an idempotent cleanup command.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant App as User application
    participant Conn as Machine Connection API
    participant Machine as Machine
    participant Resources as Resources API
    participant Manu<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="167" />_absolute(...)
    loop Until state returns to standstill
        App->>Resources: axes_groups().process_data()
        Resources-->>App: latest group positions / states
    end
    App->>App: verify final positions against target
    App->>Manual: stop_axes_group(0)
```

## Expected Result

The exact trace values can vary, but a successful run must include these pass
lines:

```text
[PASS] connect
[PASS] topology_xyzac_sp0
[PASS] start_axes_group_process_data_monitor
[PASS] axes_group_servo_on
[PASS] axes_group_enable
[PASS] enter_manual
[PASS] manual_axes_group_mcs_move_absolute
[PASS] axes_group_reached_target
[PASS] manual_axes_group_stop
[PASS] quick_start_manual_axes_group
```

The trace also prints the initial positions, computed target positions, polling
samples, and final positions. Completion is based on the axes-group state
machine returning to standstill after motion starts; position comparison is the
final result check. The target values vary across repeated runs because they are
computed from the current position at startup.

> [!NOTE]
> Commands return request receipts.
>
> `accepted` means the request passed guards and was submitted. It does not mean
> the requested state has already been reached. Observe process data or status
> monitors to verify the result.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Grouped axes are controlled through their owning axes group.
>
> Axis commands are only for ungrouped standalone axes. For an axes group, use
> MCS motion through `axes_group_id`.

## What This Uses

| Step | API |
|---|---|
| connect to a runtime | [Machine Connection API](./machine-connection.md) |
| inspect topology | [Machine Queries](./machine/queries.md) |
| power and enable a motion resource | [Machine Resources API](./machine/resources.md) |
| enter manual mode | [Mode API](./machine/mode.md) |
| start and stop manual group motion | [Manual API](./machine/manual.md) |
| observe process data | [Async Process Data](./concepts/async-process-data.md) |

# Backends

This directory owns backend integrations that bridge portable realtime `Core/`
logic to Orocos runtime targets.

Backends must map the public RT SDK contract onto reviewed realtime
implementation surfaces. They must not expose raw runtime internals directly as
the external machine API.

## Boundary

Use this order:

```text
Core (portable RT logic)
  -> backend implementation
  -> backend bridge mapping
  -> non-RT transport (OPC UA, others)
```

The public API contract is defined in `../rt-sdk/docs/src/`. The RT-side bridge
mapping is tracked in:

- `docs/backends/backend-api.md`

## Bridge Scope

The bridge maps these public SDK domains to reviewed RT implementation
surfaces:

- axis state and commands
- axes-group state and commands
- spindle state and commands
- IO state and writes
- tool state and commands
- working mode and auto control

Transport work must map to the public SDK contract through the bridge, not to
raw backend component ports/properties.

## Current Reviewed Slice

The first implemented bridge slice is `ExecutionCommandQueueBackend`.

It maps SDK-shaped automatic queue input into the Core
`ExecutionCommandQueue`:

```text
submit_axes_group_mcs_move_absolute(...)
  -> ExecutionCommandKind::axes_group_mcs_move_absolute
  -> ExecutionCommandQueue::submit(...)

submit_axes_group_mcs_trajectory(...)
  -> ExecutionCommandKind::axes_group_mcs_trajectory
  -> ExecutionCommandQueue::submit(...)
```

This class is runtime-side bridge code. It is not linked into `/rt-sdk`
production code and it does not implement OPC UA, WAMP, or Orocos transport.

The backend implementation uses C++17-compatible Core views and fixed-capacity
Core payloads. SDK-side dynamic containers are converted at this boundary before
entering the realtime queue.

`MotionBank.execution` is the current Orocos runtime consumer for the same
Core queue/executor model. It proves internal runtime dispatch, but the
SDK-to-Orocos transport boundary is still a later bridge slice.

# XYZAC + SP0 MotionBank Sim Deployment

This deployment exercises the reviewed MotionBank runtime path:

- `MachineBinding`
- `MotionBank`
- `DriveEndpointBank`
- `SimServoBank`
- `WorkingModeManager`
- `ManualExecutor`
- `AutomaticExecutor`
- `RtApi`
- global `opcua` service with component-local `rt_api.opcua_bind`

It models five logical axes `X,Y,Z,A,C` as axis IDs `0..4`, one logical
axes group `XYZAC` as group ID `0`, one logical spindle `SP0` as spindle ID
`0`, and six independent drive endpoints `0..5`.

`MotionBank` owns the Core `Axis`, axes-group membership, and Core `Spindle`
role surfaces. `MachineBinding` publishes the logical-member to endpoint
binding truth. `DriveEndpointBank` routes drive-native command and feedback
tables. `SimServoBank` owns per-endpoint simulated servo state.
`WorkingModeManager` owns the current manual/automatic/MDA working mode. `RtApi`
exposes reviewed SDK-facing services through OPC UA, reads topology from
connected `MotionBank` services, guards automatic and MDA commands through the
connected mode service, forwards queued commands to `AutomaticExecutor`, and
republishes selected public process-data streams.

## Build

From the repository root:

```bash
source "$HOME/.orocos/dev-env.sh"
cmake -S rt -B rt/build \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Debug \
  -DCMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE="$HOME/.vcpkg/scripts/buildsystems/vcpkg.cmake" \
  -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$PWD/rt/install"
cmake --build rt/build --target install -j2
```

`rt/build` is the standard RT build directory. `rt/install` is the local Orocos
import prefix used by the deployment script. Use `/tmp` for throwaway build
checks instead of adding `rt/build-*` directories.

## Run

The script assumes it is run from this directory:

```bash
cd rt/Orocos/deploy/xyzac_spindle_sim
source "$HOME/.orocos/dev-env.sh"
deployer-gnulinux -s xyzac-spindle.ops --
```

# OPC UA Backend

## Component Roles

- `opcua` global service starts and stops the OPC UA server.
- `opcua_bind` component-local service exposes reviewed RTT services.
- `meta_nc_opcua_type_transport` is the RTT transport plugin.
- `meta_nc_opcua_type_protocol` owns protocol factories and OPC UA datatype
  providers.

Do not mix these roles.

## Resource Mapping

Generated/debug OPC UA nodes follow:

```text
Objects/components/<component>/services/<service_path>/
  operations/<operation>
  properties/<property>
  attributes/<attribute>
  ports/<port>/
    value
    active
    sequence
```

String NodeIds use the same path:

```text
ns=1;s=components/<component>/services/<service_path>/operations/<operation>
```

## Service Exposure

Use service-first exposure:

```ops
component.opcua_bind.exposeService("service")
```

Do not expose a raw internal component as the public SDK API just because the
binder can traverse it. Production SDK-facing surfaces should be curated behind
reviewed `RtApi` services.

## Values

- Properties map to read/write variables when the RTT datasource is assignable.
- Attributes map to read-only live variables.
- Output ports become browseable port objects through `exposeService()`.
- Output port live values require `startPortMonitor("service/port")`.
- OPC UA clients still use normal reads/subscriptions on generated variable
  nodes.

## Operations

OPC UA method binding should use RTT operation metadata:

```text
OperationInterfacePart
  -> arity()
  -> getArgumentType(1..arity)
  -> getArgumentType(0) for result
  -> getArgumentList()
  -> OpcUaTypeProtocol converts arguments/results
```

Do not add new method support by hardcoding signatures like
`int_int_int`, `double_double_double`, or one custom struct case at a time.

Valid operation exposure requires:

- every input argument has an `OpcUaTypeProtocol`
- result is `void` or has an `OpcUaTypeProtocol`
- TaskBrowser does not show `unknown_t`
- tests cover metadata and a real OPC UA client call

## Logging

Use `opcua.LibraryLogLevel = "error"` in deploy scripts unless investigating
server internals. Valid values are `off`, `fatal`, `error`, `warning`, `info`,
`debug`, and `trace`.

# Deployer Debugging

## Environment

Use the Orocos environment before deployer or typegen checks:

```bash
source "$HOME/.orocos/dev-env.sh"
```

## Build Vs Install

Check the deploy script `path(...)` entries.

If the script loads from `rt/install/lib/orocos`, run this from the worktree
root before manual deployer testing:

```bash
cmake --build rt/build --target install -j2
```

Otherwise TaskBrowser and UaExpert may show behavior from stale installed
plugins while unit tests used fresh build-tree binaries.

## TaskBrowser Surface Checks

Use these before debugging transport layers:

```ops
ls component
ls component.service
help component.service
help component.service.operation
component.service.Attribute
component.service.Property
component.opcua_bind.lastError()
```

Use `.types` to inspect what the current RTT type system knows. If an operation
or variable shows `unknown_t`, stop and fix typekit import/generation before
debugging the backend.

## Common Manual Checks

For OPC UA demo work:

```ops
opcua.startServer()
component.opcua_bind.exposeService("service")
component.opcua_bind.exposedOperationCount()
component.opcua_bind.exposedPropertyCount()
component.opcua_bind.exposedAttributeCount()
component.opcua_bind.exposedPortCount()
component.opcua_bind.lastError()
```

For output port monitors:

```ops
component.opcua_bind.startPortMonitor("service/port_name")
component.opcua_bind.portMonitorNodeId("service/port_name")
component.opcua_bind.portMonitorIsActive("service/port_name")
```

## TaskBrowser Arguments

TaskBrowser does not pass vector operation arguments with `{...}` literals.
Create an array variable first:

```ops
var array targets = array(0.0, 1.0, 2.0)
motion_bank.axes_group.moveAxesAbsolute(0, targets, 0.5)
```

## Scripted TaskBrowser Checks

TaskBrowser `.ops` files can load the `os` helper module and use timing or
environment helpers. Prefer this for repeatable deployer smoke checks that need
small waits between command submission and status queries.

```ops
require("os")

motion_bank.axes_group.servoOn(0)
motion_bank.axes_group.enable(0)
os.millisleep(100)

rt_api.automatic.activate()
rt_api.automatic.beginSession()

var meta_nc.rt_api.AxesGroupMcsMoveAbsoluteCommandWire c
c.axes_group_id = 0
c.target_count = 5
c.targets[0] = 0.01
c.targets[1] = 0.01
c.targets[2] = 0.01
c.targets[3] = 0.01
c.targets[4] = 0.01

rt_api.automatic.submitAxesGroupMcsMoveAbsolute(c)
rt_api.automatic.cycleStart()
os.millisleep(200)

rt_api.automatic.status()
motion_bank.axes_group.latestFeedback(0)
```

Useful helpers include `os.sleep`, `os.millisleep`, `os.usleep`,
`os.nanosleep`, `os.getenv`, `os.setenv`, `os.isenv`, `os.execute`,
`os.argv`, and `os.argc`.

## Generated Files

Do not treat `.tb_history`, `orocos.log`, or mdBook output as source changes.
They are generated local artifacts.

# Prerequisites

Before running RT SDK examples, make sure the RT runtime and SDK build
dependencies are ready.

## RT Runtime

The RT SDK talks to a running realtime application. There are two supported
ways to provide that runtime.

### Approach 1: Use The Packaged Container Runtime

Use this approach when you only need an RT endpoint for SDK, NRT, HMI,
contract, or integration development.

Pull and start the published runtime image:

{{#tabs }}
{{#tab name="Docker" }}
```bash
docker pull optimalcnc/metanc-rt:latest
docker run --rm -it -p 4840:4840 optimalcnc/metanc-rt:latest
```
{{#endtab }}
{{#tab name="Podman" }}
```bash
podman pull docker.io/optimalcnc/metanc-rt:latest
podman run --rm -it -p 4840:4840 docker.io/optimalcnc/metanc-rt:latest
```
{{#endtab }}
{{#endtabs }}

This starts the maintained `xyzac_spindle_sim` RT simulation and exposes:

```text
opc.tcp://localhost:4840
```

Keep this container running while using SDK examples, manual contract runs, or
SDK-side experiments.

If you are testing local Dockerfile changes before CI publishes them, build a
local alias from the MetaNC repository root:

```bash
docker build -f docker/rt-runtime/Dockerfile -t metanc-rt .
```

Then run the local alias instead:

{{#tabs }}
{{#tab name="Docker" }}
```bash
docker run --rm -it -p 4840:4840 metanc-rt
```
{{#endtab }}
{{#tab name="Podman" }}
```bash
podman run --rm -it -p 4840:4840 localhost/metanc-rt
```
{{#endtab }}
{{#endtabs }}

For more detail, see
[RT Packaged Runtime Image](/rt/docs/getting-started/packaged-runtime.md).

### Approach 2: Install Dependencies, Build RT, And Run The Deployer

Use this approach when you need to change or validate RT source code, Orocos
components, typekits, or deployer scripts.

```bash
export REPO_ROOT="$PWD/rt"
chmod +x "$REPO_ROOT/tools/install-prerequisite.sh"
"$REPO_ROOT/tools/install-prerequisite.sh"

cd rt
source "$HOME/.orocos/dev-env.sh"
cmake --preset dev
cmake --build --preset dev
cd ..

cd rt/Orocos/deploy/xyzac_spindle_sim
deployer-gnulinux -s xyzac-spindle.ops --
```

Keep the deployer running while using the SDK.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Approach 1 consumes a ready runtime. Approach 2 proves RT source changes. If
> you change RT behavior, do not use the packaged runtime image as the only
> verification; rebuild RT locally and run the relevant RT checks.

Reference pages:

| Need | Page |
|---|---|
| run a ready RT endpoint without compiling RT | [RT Packaged Runtime Image](/rt/docs/getting-started/packaged-runtime.md) |
| install Orocos and shared developer tools | [RT Project Prerequisites](/rt/docs/appendix/project-prerequisites.md) |
| build and install the RT tree | [RT Build And Test](/rt/docs/getting-started/build-and-test.md) |
| run RT deployer scripts | [RT Run Scripts](/rt/docs/getting-started/run-scripts.md) |

# Machine Connection API

This page defines the bootstrap API that creates or returns `Machine`.

## API List

- [`connect(config)`](#connect)

<a id="connect"></a>

## `connect(config)`

Create one SDK `Machine` from connection configuration.

**Signature**

`connect(config)`

**Input**

| Field | Type | Meaning | Constraints |
|---|---|---|---|
| `backend` | `SdkBackend` | communication backend to use | supported SDK backend name; currently `OpcUa` |
| `endpoint` | `string` | backend-specific connection target | non-empty; interpreted by the selected SDK backend |
| `timeout` | `duration` | connection timeout | non-negative |

`SdkBackend` values:

| Backend | Transport | Endpoint Example |
|---|---|---|
| `OpcUa` | OPC UA | `opc.tcp://127.0.0.1:4840` |
| `sim` | reserved; not linked by the current SDK build | backend-defined |

**Return**

Return type: `ConnectResult`

| Field | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `ok` | `bool` | whether connection succeeded |
| `machine` | `Machine?` | valid machine object when `ok=true` |
| `error` | `ConnectError?` | failure details when `ok=false` |

**Failure Result**

On normal connection failure:

- `ok = false`
- `machine` is absent and invalid
- `error` is present

`ConnectError` shape:

| Field | Type | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `code` | `ConnectErrorCode` | stable failure category |
| `message` | `string` | human-readable error message |
| `stage` | `ConnectStage` | stage where connect failed |
| `retryable` | `bool` | whether retry is reasonable |

`ConnectErrorCode` values for v1:

- `invalid_config`
- `endpoint_unreachable`
- `authentication_failed`
- `timeout`
- `protocol_error`
- `version_mismatch`
- `backend_unavailable`
- `transport_error`
- `internal_error`

`ConnectStage` values for v1:

- `validate_config`
- `open_transport`
- `authenticate`
- `handshake`
- `initialize_session`

**Exception**

Exceptions are not used for normal connection failure.

Exceptions are reserved for:

- SDK misuse that violates the API contract
- unrecoverable internal runtime failure
- language-binding or runtime failure outside the normal transport contract

**Interlock**

None in RT coordination terms.

**Effects**

Creates a valid `Machine` only when `ok=true`. The SDK owns the selected
backend instance after connection.

Backend constructors and test injection seams are not public user APIs. User
code starts from `Machine::connect(config)`.

**How To Detect Result**

The caller determines success from `ok`.

- if `ok=true`, `machine` is valid
- if `ok=false`, `error` describes the failure

**Related APIs**

- [Machine API](./machine.md)
- [SDK Object Model](./public-api-shape.md)

**Examples**

{{#tabs }}
{{#tab name="C++" }}
```cpp
rt_sdk::ConnectConfig config {};
config.backend = rt_sdk::SdkBackend::OpcUa;
config.endpoint = "opc.tcp://127.0.0.1:4840";
config.timeout = std::chrono::milliseconds {2000};

auto result = rt_sdk::Machine::connect(config);
if (!result.ok) {
    // inspect result.error
}
// on success, result.machine is valid
```
{{#endtab }}
{{#tab name="Python" }}
```python
from rt_sdk import ConnectConfig, Machine, SdkBackend

config = ConnectConfig(
    backend=SdkBackend.OPCUA,
    endpoint="opc.tcp://127.0.0.1:4840",
    timeout=duration(milliseconds=2000),
)

result = Machine.connect(config)
if not result.ok:
    # inspect result.error
    pass
# on success, result.machine is valid
```
{{#endtab }}
{{#tab name="JavaScript" }}
```js
const config = {
  backend: SdkBackend.OpcUa,
  endpoint: "opc.tcp://127.0.0.1:4840",
  timeout: { milliseconds: 2000 },
};

const result = await Machine.connect(config);
if (!result.ok) {
  // inspect result.error
}
// on success, result.machine is valid
```
{{#endtab }}
{{#endtabs }}

name: systematic-debugging
description: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior, before proposing fixes

# Systematic Debugging

## Overview

Random fixes waste time and create new bugs. Quick patches mask underlying issues.

**Core principle:** ALWAYS find root cause before attempting fixes. Symptom fixes are failure.

**Violating the letter of this process is violating the spirit of debugging.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO FIXES WITHOUT ROOT CAUSE INVESTIGATION FIRST
```

If you haven't completed Phase 1, you cannot propose fixes.

## When to Use

Use for ANY technical issue:
- Test failures
- Bugs in production
- Unexpected behavior
- Performance problems
- Build failures
- Integration issues

**Use this ESPECIALLY when:**
- Under time pressure (emergencies make guessing tempting)
- "Just one quick fix" seems obvious
- You've already tried multiple fixes
- Previous fix didn't work
- You don't fully understand the issue

**Don't skip when:**
- Issue seems simple (simple bugs have root causes too)
- You're in a hurry (rushing guarantees rework)
- Manager wants it fixed NOW (systematic is faster than thrashing)

## The Four Phases

You MUST complete each phase before proceeding to the next.

### Phase 1: Root Cause Investigation

**BEFORE attempting ANY fix:**

1. **Read Error Messages Carefully**
   - Don't skip past errors or warnings
   - They often contain the exact solution
   - Read stack traces completely
   - Note line numbers, file paths, error codes

2. **Reproduce Consistently**
   - Can you trigger it reliably?
   - What are the exact steps?
   - Does it happen every time?
   - If not reproducible → gather more data, don't guess

3. **Check Recent Changes**
   - What changed that could cause this?
   - Git diff, recent commits
   - New dependencies, config changes
   - Environmental differences

<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1101" />ee if it works"
- "Add multiple changes, run tests"
- "Skip the test, I'll manually verify"
- "It's probably X, let me fix that"
- "I don't fully understand but this might work"
- "Pattern says X but I'll adapt it differently"
- "Here are the main problems: [lists fixes without investigation]"
- Proposing solutions before tracing data flow
- **"One more fix attempt" (when already tried 2+)**
- **Each fix reveals new problem in different place**

**ALL of these mean: STOP. Return to Phase 1.**

**If 3+ fixes failed:** Question the architecture (see Phase 4.5)

## your human partner's Signals You're Doing It Wrong

**Watch for these redirections:**
- "Is that not happening?" - You assumed without verifying
- "Will it show us...?" - You should have added evidence gathering
- "Stop guessing" - You're proposing fixes without understanding
- "Ultrathink this" - Question fundamentals, not just symptoms
- "We're stuck?" (frustrated) - Your approach isn't working

**When you see these:** STOP. Return to Phase 1.

## Common Rationalizations

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Issue is simple, don't need process" | Simple issues have root causes too. Process is fast for simple bugs. |
| "Emergency, no time for process" | Systematic debugging is FASTER than guess-and-check thrashing. |
| "Just try this first, then investigate" | First fix sets the pattern. Do it right from the start. |
| "I'll write test after confirming fix works" | Untested fixes don't stick. Test first proves it. |
| "Multiple fixes at once saves time" | Can't isolate what worked. Causes new bugs. |
| "Reference too long, I'll adapt the pattern" | Partial understanding guarantees bugs. Read it completely. |
| "I see the problem, let me fix it" | Seeing symptoms ≠ understanding root cause. |
| "One more fix attempt" (after 2+ failures) | 3+ failures = architectural problem. Question pattern, don't fix again. |

struct ConnectConfig {
    SdkBackend backend {SdkBackend::unsupported};
    std::string endpoint {};
    std::chrono::milliseconds timeout {std::chrono::milliseconds {2000}};
    TransportLogLevel library_log_level {TransportLogLevel::Error};
};

    none,
    invalid_config,
    endpoint_unreachable,
    authentication_failed,
    timeout,

    internal_error,
};

    return true;

ConnectResult connect_opcua(const ConnectConfig& config) {
    if (config.backend != SdkBackend::OpcUa) {
        return {false,
                {ConnectErrorCode::invalid_config,
                 "OPC UA backend requires OpcUa SDK backend",
                 ConnectStage::validate_config,
                 false},
                nullptr};
    }
    if (config.endpoint.empty()) {
        return {false,
                {ConnectErrorCode::invalid_config,
                 "SDK endpoint is empty",
                 ConnectStage::validate_config,
                 false},
                nullptr};
    }
    std::unique_ptr<OpcUaBackend> backend;
    try {
        backend = std::make_unique<OpcUaBackend>(
            OpcUaBackendConfig {config.endpoint, config.library_log_level});
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        return {false,
                {ConnectErrorCode::transport_error,
                 error.what(),
                 ConnectStage::open_transport,
                 true},
                nullptr};
    }
    return testing_connect_machine_with_backend(std::move(backend));
}

ConnectResult Machine::connect(const ConnectConfig& config) {
    if (config.backend == SdkBackend::OpcUa) {
        return connect_opcua(config);
    }
    if (config.backend == SdkBackend::sim) {
        return {false,
                {ConnectErrorCode::backend_unavailable,
                 "sim SDK backend is not linked",
                 ConnectStage::open_transport,
                 false},
                nullptr};
    }
    return {false,
            {ConnectErrorCode::invalid_config,
             "unsupported SDK backend",
             ConnectStage::validate_config,
             false},
            nullptr};
}

struct ProgramOptions {
    std::string endpoint {kDefaultEndpoint};
};

bool arg_is(const char* arg, std::string_view expected) {
    return arg != nullptr && expected == std::string_view {arg};
}

void print_usage(std::ostream& output) {
    output << "Usage:\n"
           << "  meta_nc_rt_sdk_quick_start_manual_axes_group "
              "[--endpoint opc.tcp://host:port]\n";
}

bool parse_options(int argc, char** argv, ProgramOptions& options) {
    for (int index = 1; index < argc; ++index) {
        if (arg_is(argv[index], "--endpoint")) {
            if (index + 1 >= argc) {
                std::cerr << "missing value for --endpoint\n";
                return false;
            }
            options.endpoint = argv[++index];
            continue;
        }

        if (arg_is(argv[index], "--help") || arg_is(argv[index], "-h")) {
            print_usage(std::cout);
            std::exit(0);
        }

        std::cerr << "unknown argument: " << argv[index] << '\n';
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

std::string command_status_name(meta_nc::rt_sdk::CommandStatus status) {
    using meta_nc::rt_sdk::CommandStatus;
    switch (status) {
        case CommandStatus::accepted:
            return "accepted";
        case CommandStatus::rejected_invalid_state:
            return "rejected_invalid_state";
        case CommandStatus::rejected_invalid_argument:
            return "rejected_invalid_argument";
        case CommandStatus::rejected_not_ready:
            return "rejected_not_ready";
        case CommandStatus::rejected_faulted:
            return "rejected_faulted";
        case CommandStatus::rejected_busy:
            return "rejected_busy";
    }
    return "unknown";

set -euo pipefail

OROCOS_PREFIX="${OROCOS_PREFIX:-/opt/orocos}"

if [[ -z "${METANC_RT_DEPLOY_DIR:-}" ]]; then
    echo "METANC_RT_DEPLOY_DIR is required" >&2
    exit 1
fi

if [[ -z "${METANC_RT_DEPLOY_SCRIPT:-}" ]]; then
    echo "METANC_RT_DEPLOY_SCRIPT is required" >&2
    exit 1
fi

DEPLOY_DIR="${METANC_RT_DEPLOY_DIR}"
SCRIPT_NAME="${METANC_RT_DEPLOY_SCRIPT}"

if [[ ! -f "${OROCOS_PREFIX}/env.sh" ]]; then
    echo "Missing Orocos runtime environment: ${OROCOS_PREFIX}/env.sh" >&2
    exit 1
fi

if [[ ! -d "${DEPLOY_DIR}" ]]; then
    echo "Missing MetaNC RT deploy directory: ${DEPLOY_DIR}" >&2
    exit 1
fi

# shellcheck disable=SC1090
source "${OROCOS_PREFIX}/env.sh"

    "${OROCOS_PREFIX}/toolchain/lib"
    "${OROCOS_PREFIX}/toolchain/lib/orocos/gnulinux"
    "${OROCOS_PREFIX}/toolchain/lib/orocos/gnulinux/plugins"
    "${OROCOS_PREFIX}/toolchain/lib/orocos/gnulinux/types"
    /opt/metanc/rt/install/lib
    /opt/metanc/rt/install/lib/orocos
)

while IFS= read -r -d '' dir; do
    library_paths+=("${dir}")
done < <(find /opt/metanc/rt/install/lib/orocos -type d -print0 2>/dev/null)

for dir in "${library_paths[@]}"; do
    if [[ -d "${dir}" ]]; then
        export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="${dir}${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:+:${LD_LIBRARY_PATH}}"
    fi
done

cd "${DEPLOY_DIR}"

if [[ "${1:-}" == "--check" ]]; then
    shift
    exec deployer-gnulinux --check -s "${SCRIPT_NAME}" -- "$@"
fi

if [[ "$#" -gt 0 ]]; then
    exec "$@"
fi

exec deployer-gnulinux -s "${SCRIPT_NAME}" --

# CLI reference (`scripts/image_gen.py`)

This file is for the fallback CLI mode only. Read it when the user explicitly asks to use `scripts/image_gen.py` / CLI / API / model controls, or after the user explicitly confirms that a transparent-output request should use the `gpt-image-1.5` true-transparency fallback path.

`generate-batch` is a CLI subcommand in this fallback path. It is not a top-level mode of the skill.
The word `batch` in a user request is not CLI opt-in by itself.

## What this CLI does
- `generate`: generate a new image from a prompt
- `edit`: edit one or more existing images
- `generate-batch`: run many generation jobs from a JSONL file after the user explicitly chooses CLI/API/model controls

Real API calls require **network access** + `OPENAI_API_KEY`. `--dry-run` does not.

## Quick start (works from any repo)
Set a stable path to the skill CLI (default `CODEX_HOME` is `~/.codex`):

```
export CODEX_HOME="${CODEX_HOME:-$HOME/.codex}"
export IMAGE_GEN="$CODEX_HOME/skills/.system/imagegen/scripts/image_gen.py"
```

Install dependencies into that environment with its package manager. In uv-managed environments, `uv pip install ...` remains the preferred path.

## Quick start

Dry-run (no API call; no network required; does not require the `openai` package):

```bash
python "$IMAGE_GEN" generate \
  --prompt "Test" \
  --out output/imagegen/test.png \
  --dry-run
```

Notes:
- One-off dry-runs print the API payload and the computed output path(s).
- Repo-local finals should live under `output/imagegen/`.

Generate (requires `OPENAI_API_KEY` + network):

```bash
python "$IMAGE_GEN" generate \
  --prompt "A cozy alpine cabin at dawn" \
  --size 1024x1024 \
  --out output/imagegen/alpine-cabin.png
```

Edit:

```bash
python "$IMAGE_GEN" edit \
  --image input.png \
  --prompt "Replace only the background with a warm sunset" \
<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1574" />demarks; no watermark","size":"1536x1024"}
{"prompt":"Gray wolf in profile in a snowy forest","use_case":"photorealistic-natural","composition":"eye-level","constraints":"no logos or trademarks; no watermark","size":"1024x1024"}
EOF

python "$IMAGE_GEN" generate-batch \
  --input tmp/imagegen/prompts.jsonl \
  --out-dir output/imagegen/batch \
  --concurrency 5

rm -f tmp/imagegen/prompts.jsonl
```

Notes:
- `generate-batch` requires `--out-dir`.
- generate-batch requires --out-dir.
- Use `--concurrency` to control parallelism (default `5`).
- Per-job overrides are supported in JSONL (for example `size`, `quality`, `background`, `output_format`, `output_compression`, `moderation`, `n`, `model`, `out`, and prompt-augmentation fields).
- `--n` generates multiple variants for a single prompt; `generate-batch` is for many different prompts.
- In batch mode, per-job `out` is treated as a filename under `--out-dir`.
- For many requested deliverable assets, provide one prompt/job per distinct asset and use semantic filenames when possible.

## CLI notes
- Supported sizes depend on the model. `gpt-image-2` supports flexible constrained sizes; older GPT Image models support `1024x1024`, `1536x1024`, `1024x1536`, or `auto`.
- True transparent CLI outputs require `output_format` to be `png` or `webp` and are not supported by `gpt-image-2`.
- `--prompt-file`, `--output-compression`, `--moderation`, `--max-attempts`, `--fail-fast`, `--force`, and `--no-augment` are supported.
- This CLI is intended for GPT Image models. Do not assume older non-GPT image-model behavior applies here.

## See also
- API parameter quick reference for fallback CLI mode: `references/image-api.md`
- Prompt examples shared across both top-level modes: `references/sample-prompts.md`
- Network/sandbox notes for fallback CLI mode: `references/codex-network.md`
- Built-in-first transparent image workflow: `SKILL.md` and `$CODEX_HOME/skills/.system/imagegen/scripts/remove_chroma_key.py`

+def lines(text, max_chars=22):
+    out = []
+    for raw in text.split("\n"):
+        raw = raw.strip()
+        if not raw:
+            out.append("")
+            continue
+        current = ""
+        for token in raw.split(" "):
+            if len(current) + len(token) + 1 <= max_chars:
+                current = (current + " " + token).strip()
+            else:
+                if current:
+                    out.append(current)
+                current = token
+        if current:
+            out.append(current)
+    return out
+
+
+def svg_header(title, subtitle="MetaNC 架构讨论输出 / 2026-06-24"):
+    return [

# MetaNC 架构讨论图包

日期：2026-06-24

本目录记录今天围绕 MetaNC NRT/RT 联结、`rt_gateway`、参数系统、模块边界、通信交互、可推进与可搁置项的讨论结果。

## 文件结构

- `discussion-record.md`：讨论结论记录。
- `implementation-roadmap.md`：建议推进顺序和搁置项。
- `diagrams/*.svg`：可编辑技术图源文件。
- `diagrams/*.png`：从 SVG 导出的可读图片。
- `imagegen/cover-overview.png`：gpt-image-2 生成的高质视觉总览图。
- `imagegen/prompts.md`：gpt-image-2 prompt 与参数记录。

## 推荐阅读顺序

1. `diagrams/01-overall-architecture.png`
2. `diagrams/02-module-status.png`
3. `diagrams/03-communication-interactions.png`
4. `diagrams/04-parameter-runtime-access.png`
5. `diagrams/05-roadmap-priority.png`
6. `discussion-record.md`
7. `implementation-roadmap.md`

## 核心结论

- 只加 `rt_gateway` 不够，完整模型需要同时纳入 `Parameter System / Runtime Access` 和 `Execution Policy`。
- `rt_gateway` 应是 NRT 到 RT 的通讯聚合层，普通 NRT 模块不直接持有 `rt-sdk`。
- 参数系统不应是扁平参数表，也不应吞掉 Tooling / WorkOffset；它应负责统一访问注册、描述、绑定、访问保护与 owner 分发。
- 当前可立即推进的是 `nrt/rt_gateway` 最小闭环和 ROS2 服务接口；完整参数体系、PLC 全链路、完整数据中心可分阶段推进或暂缓。

# MetaNC 架构讨论图包

日期：2026-06-24

本目录记录今天围绕 MetaNC NRT/RT 联结、`rt_gateway`、参数系统、模块边界、通信交互、可推进与可搁置项的讨论结果。

## 文件结构

- `discussion-record.md`：讨论结论记录。
- `implementation-roadmap.md`：建议推进顺序和搁置项。
- `diagrams/*.svg`：可编辑技术图源文件。
- `diagrams/*.png`：从 SVG 导出的可读图片。
- `imagegen/cover-local.png`：本地生成的第一版封面图
- `imagegen/prompts.md`：gpt-image-2 prompt 与参数记录；如需补 gpt-image-2 封面，需要明确允许向外部 API 发送精简 prompt。
- `imagegen/prompts.md`：gpt-image-2 prompt 与参数记录。

## 推荐阅读顺序

1. `diagrams/01-overall-architecture.png`
2. `diagrams/02-module-status.png`
3. `diagrams/03-communication-interactions.png`
4. `diagrams/04-parameter-runtime-access.png`
5. `diagrams/05-roadmap-priority.png`
6. `discussion-record.md`
7. `implementation-roadmap.md`

## 核心结论

- 只加 `rt_gateway` 不够，完整模型需要同时纳入 `Parameter System / Runtime Access` 和 `Execution Policy`。
- `rt_gateway` 应是 NRT 到 RT 的通讯聚合层，普通 NRT 模块不直接持有 `rt-sdk`。
- 参数系统不应是扁平参数表，也不应吞掉 Tooling / WorkOffset；它应负责统一访问注册、描述、绑定、访问保护与 owner 分发。
- 当前可立即推进的是 `nrt/rt_gateway` 最小闭环和 ROS2 服务接口；完整参数体系、PLC 全链路、完整数据中心可分阶段推进或暂缓。

# Image Generation Skill

Generates or edits images for the current project (for example website assets, game assets, UI mockups, product mockups, wireframes, logo design, photorealistic images, or infographics).

## Top-level modes and rules

This skill has exactly two top-level modes:

- **Default built-in tool mode (preferred):** built-in `image_gen` tool for normal image generation, editing, and simple transparent-image requests. Does not require `OPENAI_API_KEY`.
- **Fallback CLI mode:** `scripts/image_gen.py` CLI. Use when the user explicitly asks for the CLI/API/model path, or after the user explicitly confirms a true model-native transparency fallback with `gpt-image-1.5`. Requires `OPENAI_API_KEY`.

Within CLI fallback, the CLI exposes three subcommands:

- `generate`
- `edit`
- `generate-batch`

Rules:
- Use the built-in `image_gen` tool by default for normal image generation and editing requests.
- Do not switch to CLI fallback for ordinary quality, size, or file-path control.
- If the user explicitly asks for a transparent image/background, stay on built-in `image_gen` first: prompt for a flat removable chroma-key background, then remove it locally with the installed helper at `$CODEX_HOME/skills/.system/imagegen/scripts/remove_ch<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3467" /> try-on, person-in-scene; lock face/body/pose.
- precise-object-edit — remove/replace a specific element (including interior swaps).
- lighting-weather — time-of-day/season/atmosphere changes only.
- background-extraction — transparent background / clean cutout. Use built-in `image_gen` with chroma-key removal first for simple opaque subjects; ask before using CLI true transparency for complex subjects.
- style-transfer — apply reference style while changing subject/scene.
- compositing — multi-image insert/merge with matched lighting/perspective.
- sketch-to-render — drawing/line art to photoreal render.

## Shared prompt schema

Use the following labeled spec as shared prompt scaffolding for both top-level modes:

```text
Use case: <taxonomy slug>
Asset type: <where the asset will be used>
Primary request: <user's main prompt>
Input images: <Image 1: role; Image 2: role> (optional)
Scene/backdrop: <environment>
Subject: <main subject>
Style/medium: <photo/illustration/3D/etc>
Composition/framing: <wide/close/top-down; placement>
Lighting/mood: <lighting + mood>
Color palette: <palette notes>
Materials/textures: <surface details>
Text (verbatim): "<exact text>"
Constraints: <must keep/must avoid>
Avoid: <negative constraints>
```

Notes:
- `Asset type` and `Input images` are prompt scaffolding, not dedicated CLI flags.
- `Scene/backdrop` refers to the visual setting. It is not the same as the fallback CLI `background` parameter, which controls output transparency behavior.
- Fallback-only execution notes such as `Quality:`, `Input fidelity:`, masks, output format, and output paths belong in the CLI path only. Do not treat them as built-in `image_gen` tool arguments.

Augmentation rules:
- Keep it short.
- Add only the details needed to improve the prompt materially.
- For edits, explicitly list invariants (`change only X; keep Y unchanged`).
- If any critical detail is missing and blocks success, ask a question; otherwise proceed.

# CLI reference (`scripts/image_gen.py`)

This file is for the fallback CLI mode only. Read it when the user explicitly asks to use `scripts/image_gen.py` / CLI / API / model controls, or after the user explicitly confirms that a transparent-output request should use the `gpt-image-1.5` true-transparency fallback path.

`generate-batch` is a CLI subcommand in this fallback path. It is not a top-level mode of the skill.
The word `batch` in a user request is not CLI opt-in by itself.

## What this CLI does
- `generate`: generate a new image from a prompt
- `edit`: edit one or more existing images
- `generate-batch`: run many generation jobs from a JSONL file after the user explicitly chooses CLI/API/model controls

Real API calls require **network access** + `OPENAI_API_KEY`. `--dry-run` does not.

## Quick start (works from any repo)
Set a stable path to the skill CLI (default `CODEX_HOME` is `~/.codex`):

```
export CODEX_HOME="${CODEX_HOME:-$HOME/.codex}"
export IMAGE_GEN="$CODEX_HOME/skills/.system/imagegen/scripts/image_gen.py"
```

Install dependencies into that environment with its package manager. In uv-managed environments, `uv pip install ...` remains the preferred path.

## Quick start

Dry-run (no API call; no network required; does not require the `openai` package):

```bash
python "$IMAGE_GEN" generate \
  --prompt "Test" \
  --out output/imagegen/test.png \
  --dry-run
```

Notes:
- One-off dry-runs print the API payload and the computed output path(s).
- Repo-local finals should live under `output/imagegen/`.

Generate (requires `OPENAI_API_KEY` + network):

```bash
python "$IMAGE_GEN" generate \
  --prompt "A cozy alpine cabin at dawn" \
  --size 1024x1024 \
  --out output/imagegen/alpine-cabin.png
```

Edit:

```bash
python "$IMAGE_GEN" edit \
  --image input.png \
  --prompt "Replace only the background with a warm sunset" \

EOF

python "$IMAGE_GEN" generate-batch \
  --input tmp/imagegen/prompts.jsonl \
  --out-dir output/imagegen/batch \
  --concurrency 5

rm -f tmp/imagegen/prompts.jsonl
```

Notes:
- `generate-batch` requires `--out-dir`.
- generate-batch requires --out-dir.
- Use `--concurrency` to control parallelism (default `5`).
- Per-job overrides are supported in JSONL (for example `size`, `quality`, `background`, `output_format`, `output_compression`, `moderation`, `n`, `model`, `out`, and prompt-augmentation fields).
- `--n` generates multiple variants for a single prompt; `generate-batch` is for many different prompts.
- In batch mode, per-job `out` is treated as a filename under `--out-dir`.
- For many requested deliverable assets, provide one prompt/job per distinct asset and use semantic filenames when possible.

## CLI notes
- Supported sizes depend on the model. `gpt-image-2` supports flexible constrained sizes; older GPT Image models support `1024x1024`, `1536x1024`, `1024x1536`, or `auto`.
- True transparent CLI outputs require `output_format` to be `png` or `webp` and are not supported by `gpt-image-2`.
- `--prompt-file`, `--output-compression`, `--moderation`, `--max-attempts`, `--fail-fast`, `--force`, and `--no-augment` are supported.
- This CLI is intended for GPT Image models. Do not assume older non-GPT image-model behavior applies here.

## See also
- API parameter quick reference for fallback CLI mode: `references/image-api.md`
- Prompt examples shared across both top-level modes: `references/sample-prompts.md`
- Network/sandbox notes for fallback CLI mode: `references/codex-network.md`
- Built-in-first transparent image workflow: `SKILL.md` and `$CODEX_HOME/skills/.system/imagegen/scripts/remove_chroma_key.py`

# Image API quick reference

This file is for the fallback CLI mode only. Use it when the user explicitly asks to use `scripts/image_gen.py` / CLI / API / model controls, or after the user explicitly confirms that a transparent-output request should use the `gpt-image-1.5` true-transparency fallback path.

These parameters describe the Image API and bundled CLI fallback surface. Do not assume they are normal arguments on the built-in `image_gen` tool.

## Scope
- This fallback CLI is intended for GPT Image models (`gpt-image-2`, `gpt-image-1.5`, `gpt-image-1`, and `gpt-image-1-mini`).
- The built-in `image_gen` tool and the fallback CLI do not expose the same controls.

## Model summary

| Model | Quality | Input fidelity | Resolutions | Recommended use |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `gpt-image-2` | `low`, `medium`, `high`, `auto` | Always high fidelity for image inputs; do not set `input_fidelity` | `auto` or flexible sizes that satisfy the constraints below | Default for new CLI/API workflows: high-quality generation and editing, text-heavy images, photorealism, compositing, identity-sensitive edits, and workflows where fewer retries matter |
| `gpt-image-1.5` | `low`, `medium`, `high`, `auto` | `low`, `high` | `1024x1024`, `1024x1536`, `1536x1024`, `auto` | True transparent-background fallback and backward-compatible workflows |
| `gpt-image-1` | `low`, `medium`, `high`, `auto` | `low`, `high` | `1024x1024`, `1024x1536`, `1536x1024`, `auto` | Legacy compatibility |
| `gpt-image-1-mini` | `low`, `medium`, `high`, `auto` | `low`, `high` | `1024x1024`, `1024x1536`, `1536x1024`, `auto` | Cost-sensitive draft batches and lower-stakes previews |

## gpt-image-2 sizes

`gpt-image-2` accepts `auto` or any `WIDTHxHEIGHT` size that satisfies all constraints:

- Maximum edge length must be less than or equal to `3840px`.
- Both edges must be mul<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="546" />eter. The skill's default transparent-image path is built-in `image_gen` with a flat chroma-key background, followed by local alpha extraction with `python "${CODEX_HOME:-$HOME/.codex}/skills/.system/imagegen/scripts/remove_chroma_key.py"`.

Use CLI `gpt-image-1.5` with `background=transparent` and a transparent-capable output format such as `png` or `webp` only after the user explicitly confirms that fallback, unless they already requested `gpt-image-1.5`, `scripts/image_gen.py`, or CLI fallback. If the user asks for true/native transparency, the subject is too complex for clean chroma-key removal, or local background removal fails validation, explain the tradeoff and ask before switching.

## Output
- `data[]` list with `b64_json` per image
- The bundled `scripts/image_gen.py` CLI decodes `b64_json` and writes output files for you.

## Limits and notes
- Input images and masks must be under 50MB.
- Use the edits endpoint when the user requests changes to an existing image.
- Masking is prompt-guided; exact shapes are not guaranteed.
- Large sizes and high quality increase latency and cost.
- Use `quality=low` for fast drafts, thumbnails, and quick iterations. Use `medium` or `high` for final assets, dense text, diagrams, identity-sensitive edits, or high-resolution outputs.
- High `input_fidelity` can materially increase input token usage on models that support it.
- If a request fails because a specific option is unsupported by the selected GPT Image model, retry manually without that option only when the option is not required by the user. If true transparent CLI output is required, ask before switching to `gpt-image-1.5` instead of dropping `background=transparent`, unless the user already explicitly chose that fallback.

## Important boundary
- `quality`, `input_fidelity`, explicit masks, `background`, `output_format`, and related parameters are fallback-only execution controls.
- Do not assume they are built-in `image_gen` tool arguments.
